## Chapter 36.

### Concerning Lukewarm Society and True Brotherhood.

§. 1. The lukewarm Laodicea <note>A reference to the church in Laodicea from Revelation 3:14-22, symbolizing a complacent or "half-hearted" Christian community</note>—which, despite all its imagined wealth, is wretched, miserable, poor, blind, and naked—has become unaccustomed to righteous rebuke and discipline. It lives without Christian zeal or a change of heart <note>original: "Gemüths-Aenderung," implying a fundamental internal transformation or repentance</note>, crying out "Lord, Lord," and carrying the blood of atonement only in the mouth or upon the tongue. It boasts and parades its external, elemental baptism, the Lord's Supper, love-feasts <note>term: "Liebes-Mahlen" (Agape feasts), communal meals shared by early Christians</note>, colleges <note>term: "Collegien," likely referring to the 'Collegia Pietatis' or private devotional circles common in Pietism</note>, assemblies, disputations, and more. Yet, it will *not* open the heart to the knocking voice of the Word, nor will it let in the *Jesus who stands at the door*, who provides full nourishment for eternal life, and who comes *to make all things new*. He desires to hold the Supper with them substantially, without any figurative imagery.

§. 2. These babblers <note>original: "Schwätzer," a derogatory term for those who talk much of religion without practicing it</note> sin grievously against the wounds of the Lord, about which they chatter much without awe or shame, while in reality they continue to live their lives unhealed.

§. 5. Vengeance and judgment belong so properly to the <term>Regalia of God</term> <note>original: "Regalien GOttes"; royal prerogatives or rights belonging exclusively to a sovereign</note> that no evangelical Christian is permitted to lay hands upon them. For otherwise, he would not only deny **the Cross** and **reject** the suffering—which teaches obedience and leads to perfection—but he would also certainly act unjustly. In doing so, he would remove himself from Grace to place himself under the Law, and instead of the ways, thoughts, and judgments of God, he would choose **human** ones. It is therefore said with good reason:
> Vengeance is not yours to take; **vengeance is mine**, says the Lord; Judge not, etc. <note>A synthesis of Romans 12:19 and Matthew 7:1</note>

But those who stand under the Law shall be judged fairly according to the Law, and all the lawless shall be rightfully punished without the Law. On the other hand, those who **have achieved a complete union of their will with God, and are perfectly righteous or spiritual beings**, are in such a state that the Lord decides all things through them; and they, in turn, are judged by no one—unless someone wished to judge God, <margin>Note Well. Note Well. Note Well.</margin> **who reigns** as King **within them**.

<note>The author uses "evangelical" here in its original sense: pertaining to the Gospel (Evangelium) rather than a specific modern denomination.</note>

# Chapter 40
## The Speech of Wisdom to the Lamb, the Daughters of Zion, and Her Hidden Friend.

§. 1. Your name is indeed more precious than the power of balsam, ointment, and oil; why then will you not revive and refresh the hidden virgins with your lovely Gospel and your powerful kisses of love? O you King of Kings, draw my beloved powerfully into your hidden chambers, so that we may rejoice in you and be your constant followers.

§. 2. But you daughters of Jerusalem, why has my black color of the cross <note>The "blackness" refers to the "I am black but comely" motif from the Song of Solomon, representing outward suffering or humility.</note> become so hated? Why do the children of my mother rage and why have they become so angry? Must I not guard my vineyard, and not for your sake watch over the friends, who are forsaken because of their great deviations? Can you now show me a single successor who is the true martial hero of victory <note>original: "martialische Sieges-Held"</note>, or the champion of God <note>original: "Kämpfer GOttes," likely referring to the name Israel</note>, and say where he is to be found? Do you think that I shall forever and eternally go around among all nations, be restless, seek rest, be veiled, and stand before the Lord as one who wanders?

<summary>Divine Wisdom addresses the King of Kings and the Daughters of Jerusalem, pleading for spiritual intimacy while questioning why the suffering and "blackness" of the cross are despised by the world.</summary>

## Chapter 41.

### Concerning the New Heaven and the New Earth.

Section 1. The entirety of the new heaven and the entire new earth are characterized by the divine attributes of the Lamb <note>A common mystical reference to Christ</note> and the Virgins <note>Likely referring to the "wise virgins" or pure souls who follow the Lamb</note>, or rather, they bear their image and likeness. They qualify <term>qualificiren: to interact or harmonize through shared essential qualities</term> incessantly with one another, and are inseparably paired and linked together. <margin><image-desc>A six-pointed star symbol (hexagram).</image-desc></margin> The water of this new earth is a joyful, fiery, lovely, moist, and transparent water; its fire is lovely, joyful, moist, bright, and glorious. The air is likewise refreshing, volatile, clear, subtle, and pleasant beyond measure. The earth is also transparent, soft, fruitful, and very majestic, adorned and clothed with indescribable fruits. The inhabitants thereof are so magnificent that Solomon in all his glory was never arrayed in any garment like *a single one* of these.

# On the Warning Against False Security

§. 2. The visible waters contain various kinds of water within them and serve as a covering for the invisible ones. The same condition applies to the remaining elements. No one, in their departure from temporality <note>The state of living within time/the physical world</note>, can progress further than their *mind* extends through faith. Oh, that the *children of men were wise* and would consider such things <margin>Note:</margin> then the secure, worldly life would cease! No one can describe the *anguish and torment* which is to be found in the deep bowels and central life-forces of the elements. (Compare James 5:3 <note>original: "conf. Jac. 5, 3." A biblical reference to the "rust" of riches witnessing against the soul</note>.) One can see this reflected sufficiently in the departure of many believers who are helped through these prisons during the throes of death; <margin>Note:</margin> those, however, who have *completely overcome* have experienced this during their lifetime. On the other hand, those who refuse to accept faith must become aware of this all too late.

§. 3. Many have become so hardened through the hardening of their own sins that they cannot be dissolved in the region of the first death <margin>Note:</margin> they must therefore go with the *devils into the second death* <note>A theological term for eternal separation from God or final judgment</note>. Therefore, when one prays for the whole human race in general, it must be done with this condition: That the Lord would help all in such a way as they are capable of being helped.

The Lamb and Wisdom, in their divine embodiment, always demonstrate a sincere and earnest dove-like simplicity and love; thus, practiced senses can easily distinguish the disguised Woman of Folly with her dragon-like <margin>(b)</margin> lamb and her whorish, loathsome, and inconstant fluttering spirits. <note>The "Woman of Folly" and the "dragon-like lamb" are references to the deceptive spiritual forces described in the Book of Revelation and Proverbs.</note>

§. 13. God created the invisible holy worlds with delight; therefore, *the morning stars* praised Him. This visible world, which in its original essence was brought into disorder by the fallen angels, He created <margin>m:</margin> *with labor*; <note>original: "mit Mühe"</note> therefore *He rested on the seventh day* from all His works which He had made. Fallen man, who again separated what God had joined together, He redeemed in Christ *with bloody labor*; *for this reason, He shall reign with His faithful followers for a thousand years upon the earth.* The lost *adversaries* He will bring back in the fire of His zeal; therefore, the *Israel of God* <note>A term for the community of true believers.</note> has kept its years of Fire and Jubilee. The days of creation were six; the time of redemption—from the moment the promise was given in Paradise until the complete victory in the Kingdom of Christ on earth—shall be 6,000 years, excluding the days of shortening; the

<meta>catchword: Rest-</meta>

<summary>This page discusses the discernment of divine wisdom versus spiritual deception and outlines a millenarian chronology where 6,000 years of "labor" lead to Christ's thousand-year reign.</summary>
<keywords>divine wisdom, creation, redemption, 6000 years, millennium, morning stars, spiritual discernment, Israel of God</keywords>

--- Page 372 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion of divine cycles and the eventual restoration of all things, following the previous page's focus on the nature of time and judgment.</meta>

# Chapter 42. Concerning the [Restoration]

The day of rest was the seventh. Every seventh year was a year of rest and celebration, and when seven times seven, or 49 years, had passed, the 50th followed, which was a Jubilee and Year of Rejoicing <note>original: "Jubel- und Hall-Jahr." The "Hall-Jahr" refers to the sounding of the trumpet or "shofar" to announce the liberation of slaves and return of property.</note>. How many such Jubilee years there will be, no one can determine; rather, God alone knows how many of them there shall be and are necessarily required before all slaves can come to the freedom of the Son. Yet it remains infallible that a thousand years before the Lord are but a day, and a day is like a thousand years; thus, the thousand years after every 6,000—or the seventh millennium—and the thousand years after the seven times seven thousand years (which follows the 49,000th and is the 50,000th) are special Sabbaths, years of peace and celebration, of which nothing more is to be said now.

§. 14. How much longer will people spare sin, be merciful toward evil, and in such a manner exclude Christ, who came so that he might destroy the devil and all his works, and his entire evil nature?

§. 15. Whoever sins while relying on the mercy of God shall be punished with the justice of God; for the Lord does <margin>Note:</margin> not allow himself to be mocked, but is zealous and holy. All unbelievers and the godless will one day be swept away with the broom of destruction, and the sun of the righteous shall at last no longer set, nor their moon lose its glow, and the days of suffering shall have an end.

<meta>catchword: After</meta>

<summary>The author discusses the mystical significance of the Jubilee year and the 50,000-year cycle of restoration, warning that God's mercy should not be used as an excuse for continued sin.</summary>
<keywords>Jubilee year, Sabbath, millennium, divine justice, restoration of all things, 50,000-year cycle, holiness</keywords>

# Post-Remembrance
<note>German: "Nach-Erinnerung" – a concluding reflection or postscript.</note>

---

<margin>Matthew</margin>
<margin>10:</margin>
<margin>M: M: B:</margin>
§. 1. From the days of John the Baptist until now, the Kingdom of Heaven *breaks through with force*, and those who exercise force, or *the forceful breakers-through, seize it for themselves*. <note>A reference to Matthew 11:12 regarding the spiritual intensity required to enter the Kingdom.</note> Thus the Lord spoke in the days of his humiliation: what then will not have occurred since the Ascension of Christ, and after his coming in revelations to the Evangelist John on the Island of Patmos. At the full manifestation of *his second coming*, he will send his angels to *gather the elect together from the four winds of the world*; and at the completion of the rule of this world, he will send out his angels to collect all offenses, and those who have acted unjustly, *out of his Kingdom*; indeed, *the wicked will then be completely separated and cast out from the midst of the righteous* into the *fiery furnace, where there will be wailing and gnashing of teeth*. At that same time, *the righteous will also shine forth and glow like the sun in the Kingdom of their Father*. Whoever has ears to hear, let him hear, and do not despise this *time of your visitation*, as Jerusalem did in former times; for they did not recognize what served their peace, but rather *killed the Prince of Life*. <note>original: "Herzog des Lebens" – literally 'Duke' or 'Leader' of life, a common archaic German title for Christ.</note>

§. 2. He who does wrong, offends, and causes harm, let him do more, even more, and further wrong. And he who makes himself filthy and is filthy, let him make himself more, even more, and further filthy. And he who is righteous, let him become more righteous, and continue to do more righteousness; and he who is holy, let him become more, even more, and further holy. And behold, I (a) come quickly, and my reward is with me, to repay every man according to what his work shall be. <note>This is a translation of Revelation 22:11-12, suggesting that at the end of days, people's spiritual states become fixed or intensified.</note> What else does the prophecy intend to indicate by this, other than that the godless increase in godlessness, and will lead a godless existence in ever higher degrees (b)? And that some depart from the faith and wallow in all kinds of impurity, in unbelief, sluggishness, negligence, despondency, in fornications, murders, abominations; in idolatry, sorcery, lies, and all kinds of sins. The Spirit of Christ also gives us to understand through this that many are purified, made white, proven, and prepared for the coming of Jesus Christ, so that they may be fit to escape all that—which shall happen—to stand before the Son of Man and to endure His majestic glory.

Since in the <term>Formulas</term> <note>original: "Formulis"; likely referring to the standard catechisms or liturgical templates of the time</note> the second commandment of the Lord has been entirely omitted, and two have been made out of the tenth commandment; and since the lawlessness of the ungodly, the cooling of the love of the pious, and the sleepiness of the ten virgins <note>A biblical reference to the Parable of the Ten Virgins in Matthew 25, symbolizing spiritual unpreparedness</note> also increases more and more; therefore the ten commandments of God, the renewed law of the Lord, and a short Evangelical Confession of Faith are further appended here.

# The Ten Commandments of God.

### The First Commandment.

I am the Lord your God; you shall have no other gods before my face. <note>The phrase "before my face" is a literal translation of the German "vor meinem Angesicht," emphasizing God's omnipresence.</note>

### The Second Commandment.

You shall not make for yourself any carved image or any likeness of anything that is above in heaven, or that is below on the earth, or that is in the water under the earth.
You shall not bow down before them nor serve them. For I, the Lord your God, am a jealous <note>original: "eifriger," which can mean zealous or jealous in a protective sense</note> God, visiting the iniquity of the fathers upon the children, unto the third and fourth generation of those who hate me; and showing mercy to many thousands, namely to those who love me and keep my commandments.

### The Third Commandment.

You shall not take the name of the Lord your God in vain. <note>The German numbering follows the Lutheran tradition, which combines the prohibition of images into the first/second commandment and splits the tenth commandment into two.</note>

<summary>This page presents the first three commandments of the Decalogue, emphasizing the exclusivity of worship and the prohibition of idolatry and the misuse of God's name.</summary>
<keywords>Ten Commandments, Decalogue, Idolatry, Divine Law, Lutheran tradition, Mercy, Iniquity</keywords>

--- Page 381 ---
<meta>This page continues the listing of the Ten Commandments, following the prohibition against taking the Lord's name in vain.</meta>

->❀ ) o ( ❀<-

for the Lord will not leave him unpunished who uses His name in vain. <note>This concludes the third commandment regarding the misuse of God's name.</note>

### The Fourth Commandment.

Remember the Sabbath day, that you keep it holy.

### The Fifth Commandment.

You shall honor your father and your mother, so that your days may be lengthened in the land that I, the Lord your God, have given to you.

### The Sixth Commandment.

You shall not kill.

### The Seventh Commandment.

You shall not commit adultery.

### The Eighth Commandment.

You shall not steal.

### The Ninth Commandment.

You shall not bear false witness against your neighbor.

## The Tenth Commandment.

You shall not covet your neighbor's house. You shall not covet your neighbor's wife, nor his manservant, nor his maidservant, nor his ox, nor his donkey, nor anything that belongs to your neighbor.

> Deuteronomy 6:4–5. Hear, O Israel: The Lord our God is one Lord. And you shall love the Lord your God with all your heart, with all your soul, and with all your strength. <note>The original German "Vermögen" implies one's entire capacity or means.</note>

> Numbers 6:24–26. The Lord bless you and keep you! The Lord make His face shine upon you and be gracious to you! The Lord lift up His countenance upon you and give you peace. <note>This is the Priestly Blessing, traditionally used at the conclusion of many Christian and Jewish services.</note>

> Chapter 10:35–36. Lord, arise, let Your enemies be scattered, and let those who hate You flee before You! Return, O Lord...

<margin>page 83.</margin> If we could demonstrate goodness toward our enemies and persecutors out of love, then one could possess patience and love, both within oneself and toward others.

--- Page 398 ---
<meta>This page continues the author's discourse on spiritual discernment, moving from the nature of deceptive spirits to the internal process of divine transformation and the dangers of rationalist theology.</meta>

<margin>page: 132.</margin> Sorcerers <note>German: Zauberer; here referring to those who manipulate spiritual forces through dark arts</note> can appear to be present with their physical bodies—sitting, walking, working, and acting—while their inner self, as the most noble part, flies here and there to attend assemblies of witches. However, they must remain entirely untouched in that place where God dwells in Nature <note>The hexagram symbol with a central dot often represents the "Seal of Solomon" or the heart of the macrocosm in alchemical and mystical traditions</note>. And because they, as children of Belial <note>A biblical term for the personification of worthlessness or the devil</note>, know very well how to disguise themselves, they may even pass for the most distinguished administrators of Grace among the inexperienced; thus, the light of the Holy Spirit is necessary to be able to test such spirits.

<margin>page: 135.</margin> That God, amidst much anxiety, distress, and oppression, makes a path for Himself in the heart; and after great fear and dignity, He finally transforms the Valley of Tears into a Fountain of Love.

<margin>page: 139.</margin> Satan has now arranged his household in such a way that if one avoids one net, he may be caught in another; that is, he initially comes quite subtly, bringing to the children of men—either through their own reason or through the writings of other spirits—doubts about what appears unnecessary or contradictory in the Holy Scriptures. Consequently, he suggests that such things cannot be God's Word, leading them to doubt the events that occurred—as well as the Creation, the fall of Lucifer and Adam. Finally, the evil spirit robs man of his anchor and very best comfort: the Work of Redemption of our Lord Jesus. He instills in man's reason questions such as: how a virgin could <unclear>conceive</unclear> beyond the course of nature—how such a man, who was treated so cruelly and finally died lingering on the Cross <note>represented by the cross symbol</note>, could also be at once the Son of God and true God. To him <note>the rationalist</note>, this is impossible; thus he denies the Bible and all the truths contained therein, claiming there are no spirits and no devil. He makes himself quite secure, forming for himself a God of Nature, but one who is deaf, dumb, and without feeling, with whom he can deal as he pleases. This, alas! is today's "New Theology" <note>A critique of Enlightenment rationalism and Deism which sought to strip Christianity of its supernatural elements</note>.

<margin>page: 146.</margin> The first call of the voice of repentance sounds: "Change yourselves <note>or "Repent"</note> and believe in the Gospel." The second: "Come and follow me"—and "if thou wilt be perfect, go thy way, buy all—renounce all." Likewise: "A soldier in a campaign does not entangle himself in the affairs of providing for his livelihood," and so forth.

<summary>The author warns against the deceptive nature of sorcerers and the "New Theology" of rationalism, which uses human reason to deny the divinity of Christ and the reality of the spirit world. He emphasizes that true spiritual progress requires the light of the Holy Spirit and a total renunciation of worldly entanglements.</summary>

<keywords>sorcery, discernment of spirits, Belial, Holy Spirit, rationalism, New Theology, doubt, Redemption, Jesus Christ, repentance, spiritual warfare</keywords>

<margin>p. 188.</margin> Concerning false resignation, or a false "nothingness" <note>A warning against a passive state of mind that mimics true spiritual surrender but lacks genuine divine connection</note>.

# Page 308. On the Shell of Formal Christians and the Kernel of Rebirth

### Page 310.
Very important questions are presented to those who wish to be considered worthy communicants <note>term: Comunicanten; those authorized to receive Holy Communion</note> of the Lord—whereby their <unclear>fixed</unclear> statutes are held up before them. On the contrary: the elect would be enlightened by the divine loveliness as if in a bright flash of lightning—and that the Lord Jesus, with His heavenly Father in the Holy Spirit, wishes to come to those who love Him and hold the Last Supper with them, etc.: this is very much to be noted!

### Page 312.
The forceful ones seized the Kingdom for themselves, so that its future truly happened among them, and the heavenly delicacies <note>original: "Delicatessen"; here meaning spiritual nourishment or divine graces</note> were essentially eaten and drunk.

**Section 10.** No one should think that the godless can teach rightly; rather, believe with certainty that his words are nothing but impure spirits which cover themselves with the text of Holy Scripture. God has no need of him; and how should a servant of the devil and a slave to sin establish peace between God and men, or proclaim the Gospel? If the Lord were to make use of the godless in such a way, then the Apostolic Calling and the New Birth <note>term: Wiedergeborth; the Pietist concept of a radical internal transformation</note> would not be necessary.

### Page 315.
How man is so entirely indifferent in regard to his eternal well-being or woe.

### Page 322.
That the Lord of Hosts and King of all Kings shall certainly yet take up His Kingdom on Earth; therein He shall reign royally, and the power under the whole heaven would belong to Him.

### Page 328.
How King Solomon indeed fell, but also rose up from it again—therefore, no one can highly prize the preciousness of God's goodness if he has not had to do without it for a time.

**Section 7.** If nature is the center of the soul, the soul the center of the spirit, and the spirit the center of the Father of Spirits, who is the Center of Centers <note>original: "Centrum Centrorum"; a mystical term for the ultimate divine source or God</note>.

October 20, 1787. at the end:</margin> That now only the <term>violent ones<note>original: "Gewaltübende"; a reference to Matthew 11:12, suggesting those who exert great spiritual effort or "force" to enter the kingdom.</note></term> or the powerful breakers-through would seize the kingdom of heaven for themselves—At the full manifestation of the <term>Second Coming<note>original: "2ten Zukunft"; literally the "Second Future" or Advent.</note></term> of our Lord Jesus, He will send His angels to gather the elect together from the four winds of the world—Then all offenses shall be banished from this kingdom, and the wicked shall be completely separated and cast out from the midst of the righteous into the furnace of fire, where there will be wailing and gnashing of teeth. At that same time (: which is perhaps near at hand :) the righteous shall also shine forth in the kingdom of their Father and glisten like the sun. Yes! Lord Jesus, come quickly. Amen.

<summary>The author warns that the doctrine of universal restoration can lead to spiritual laziness and damnation, concluding with a fervent prayer for the Second Coming and the final purification of the righteous.</summary>
<keywords>Restoration of All Things, Universalism, Damnation, Second Coming, Judgment, Salvation, Spiritual Discipline, End Times</keywords>
